### R1. TERMINAL POPULATION INTERACTION / SELECTOR CORRELATION
Status: LIVE.
Historical anchor: Stage29 J12-POP-INTERACTION = GREEN.
This is currently the most goal-near relative formulation, but it is not yet proved.
Exact populations:
M2 = exactly two faces, no space condition
N2 = exactly two faces + space
M3 = three faces
P  = three faces + space
Selected two-face incidence counts:
I2   = M2 + 3*M3
I2^S = N2 + 3*P.
Define
a(B) = N2/M2,
r(B) = M3/M2,
b(B) = P/M3,
s(B) = I2^S/I2.
Then exactly
s = (a + 3*r*b)/(1+3*r),
and therefore
s-a = 3*r*(b-a)/(1+3*r).
Since the audited Stage24 theorem gives
a(B)=N2/M2 -> 0,
we obtain the exact equivalence:
### Theorem 4-A — TerminalSelectorCorrelationEquivalence
P/M3 -> 0
iff
P/M3 - N2/M2 -> 0,
iff
(s-a)/r -> 0.
Thus the final M3 ratio can be closed by proving that the space-square survival rate on the third-face-complete stratum does not separate from the exactly-two-face space survival rate by an O(1) amount.
This is not an independence assumption. It is an exact receiver reformulation.
A weaker multiplicative receiver is also sufficient. Define
T_terminal(B)
 =
(P/M3)/(N2/M2).
The certified upper
N2/M2 <<_epsilon B^(-1/2+epsilon)*(log B)^(-5)
implies:
if for some delta>0,
T_terminal(B) << B^(1/2-delta),
then P/M3 -> 0.
In particular a subpolynomial terminal interaction bound
T_terminal(B)=B^o(1)
would suffice.
#### Existing progress
Stage29 already proves the legal survival ladder:
I1^S/I1
 ~ (kappa*pi/18)*(log B)^2/B,
B^(-3/4)*(log B)^(-5)
 << I2^S/I2
 <<_epsilon B^(-1/2+epsilon)*(log B)^(-5),
and
P/(M2+M3)
 <<_epsilon B^(-1/2+epsilon)*(log B)^(-5).
This is the only historical Stage29 attack route with GREEN theorem credit.
#### Existing geometric support
Stage28 placed the two competing completion conditions over the same exact two-face host
Y=Bl_4(P1 x P1)
with the same physical anticanonical height.
Both are degree-two K3 covers and both branch divisors have class -2K_Y.
Coarse invariants therefore cancel:
- same base
- same cover degree
- same total branch class
- same K3 canonical type
- same physical base-height line bundle.
The first real geometric difference is:
space cover branch      = 4 genus-0 components
third-face cover branch = 2 genus-1 components.
Good-prime first-order local sieve dimensions also agree; the relative local product has no polynomial or first-order logarithmic drift.
Hence the surviving Stage28 gate was already:
same-measure selector correlation / centered covariance / marginal comparison,
not direct joint endpoint counting.
StructureRadar's closest historical receiver was SR-STR-169 same-measure selector correlation, still an external gate.
#### Exact centered-covariance normal form
Let Omega_B be the selected two-face incidence host of size
I2=M2+3*M3.
Define Boolean selectors on Omega_B:
T=1 iff the third face is integral,
S=1 iff the space diagonal is integral.
Then exactly
E[T] = rho = 3*M3/I2,
E[S] = s = (N2+3*P)/I2,
E[S*T] = 3*P/I2.
Therefore
Cov(S,T)
 = E[S*T]-E[S]E[T]
 = 3*(P*M2-M3*N2)/(M2+3*M3)^2.
Also
Var(T)
 = rho*(1-rho)
 = 3*M2*M3/(M2+3*M3)^2.
Hence:
### Theorem 4-B — TerminalSelectorCovarianceIdentity
Cov(S,T)/Var(T)
 =
P/M3 - N2/M2.
Equivalently, with the 2x2 contingency determinant
Delta_terminal(B):=P*M2-M3*N2,
the M3 goal satisfies
P/M3 -> 0
iff
Delta_terminal(B)=o(M2*M3),
because N2/M2->0 is already audited.
Thus the sharp R1 receiver is
Cov(S,T)=o(Var(T)).
This is weaker and more tailored than demanding a generic fixed-power covariance deficit relative to the entire selected-incidence host.
The historical SR-STR-169 gate is therefore NOT directly imported: it was proved/reduced on a different Stage27 physical measure and targeted a stronger whole-host correlation deficit.
However the changed receiver makes a bounded rematch legal:
NEW_R1_REENTRY_REASON=RARE_SELECTOR_RELATIVE_COVARIANCE_RECEIVER.
The exact adapter question is now:
> Can the terminal centered selector pair (S,T) on the Stage29 selected-two-face
> physical host be decomposed into one of the already-classified same-measure
> correlation architectures with coefficient/energy normalization relative to
> Var(T), while preserving primitive/canonical/R<=B semantics?
No cross-measure promotion is allowed.
#### Current blocker
Need a theorem on the exact primitive/canonical selected-two-face physical measure proving one of:
1. P/M3 - N2/M2 -> 0;
2. T_terminal(B)=B^o(1);
3. any explicit interaction growth < B^(1/2-delta);
4. equivalently a centered selector-covariance estimate strong enough to imply the above.
This route is genuinely more top-down than terminal-cover geometry.
---
### R2. SAME-M3 LOCAL-TO-GLOBAL / MASTER-HIT PHYSICAL TRANSFER
Status: LIVE_BLOCKED_ON_GLOBAL_TRANSFER.
Historical kernel: K16-C3-M3-LOCAL-TO-GLOBAL.
Exact assets already proved:
- global Master-Hit coverage of every primitive Euler brick;
- exact primitive/canonical physical population semantics;
- local squareclass laws;
- finite-modulus character cancellation;
- fixed finite-prime-set CRT extension;
- a growing-sieve theorem on the parameter/local side.
The original wall survived:
parameter/local equidistribution
  !=
exact physical M3 measure under R<=B.
The main reason is the moving gcd / physical-height normalization and the conditioning on the third-face square.
Later ARCHIVE work repaired much of the geometry:
- exact odd-edge divisor coordinates;
- one-object/one-count physical parametrization;
- exact primitivity;
- exact R<=B inequality;
- integral bidegree-(2,2) model;
- signed squarefree delta decomposition;
- only subpolynomially many squareclass allocations per physical packet.
But no uniform fixed-q / moving-delta count was proved with an error summable over the exact M3 population.
#### Current blocker
A same-measure theorem of one of these types:
- fixed-q equidistribution on exact physical M3;
- relative large sieve on exact M3;
- uniform delta-slice counting with summable error;
- physical weighted character-sum theorem preserving primitivity, canonicalization and multiplicity.
This remains a core route, not superseded by R1.
---
### R2b. PHYSICAL DIVISOR / DELTA DESCENT
Status: LIVE_SUPPORT_ROUTE under R2.
This is not a separate goal. It is the strongest exact physical implementation currently available for R2.
Reached:
- exact odd-edge divisor coordinates;
- exact primitive criterion;
- exact third-face equation;
- exact physical height;
- plus/minus twin model;
- common signed squarefree kernel delta;
- delta_odd | rad(PQ);
- subpolynomial squareclass-allocation count;
- each fixed-delta equation separately genus 0;
- coupled slice retains a genus-1 core.
Unfinished:
- fixed-q relative counting uniform in delta/cross-gcd labels;
- aggregation over all physical delta packets.
Reopen/continue only if the next step is an actual same-measure counting estimate, not another coordinate rewrite.
---
### R3. K3 ELLIPTIC FIBRATION / MOVING-BASE DISTRIBUTION
Status: LIVE_HIGH_VALUE_EXTERNAL_GATE.
Reached:
- exact identification of Euler K3 K_c;
- explicit rational fibration t=a2/a3;
- explicit genus-one / elliptic model;
- physical height formula;
- explicit duplication law;
- proof that one fixed rank-positive fiber contributes only subpolynomially in B;
- fixed-t uniform anti-concentration:
  N_t(B)=B^o(1);
- therefore at least B^(1/3-o(1)) occupied t-values;
- second transverse fibration and an exact degree-2 quotient map between fibrations;
- 2-isogeny/descent checked; physically relevant class returns to the original quartic and gives no shortcut.
Negative conclusions already established:
- high rank on one fiber cannot explain global M3 growth;
- bounded individual-fiber Chabauty/MW work is not global coverage;
- fixed-fiber density scans do not replace a moving-base theorem.
#### Weaker average receiver — endpoint fiber energy
For each occupied rational base value t define
m_t(B):=#{E in M3(B): t(E)=t},
p_t(B):=#{E in P(B): t(E)=t}.
Then exactly
M3(B)=sum_t m_t(B),
P(B)=sum_t p_t(B),
and
P/M3
 =
sum_t [m_t/M3] * [p_t/m_t],
with the ratio defined as 0 when m_t=0.
Thus the M3 target requires only weighted average decay of the fiber endpoint ratio; uniform endpoint emptiness on every moving fiber is much stronger than necessary.
There is also an exact two-copy energy receiver.
By Cauchy-Schwarz,
P(B)^2
 <=
M3(B)
*
sum_t p_t(B)^2/m_t(B).
Therefore:
### Receiver R3-A — MovingFiberEndpointEnergy
It is sufficient to prove
sum_t p_t(B)^2/m_t(B)
 =
o(M3(B)).
Equivalently, if q_t:=p_t/m_t and w_t:=m_t/M3,
sum_t w_t*q_t^2 -> 0
is sufficient.
A good/bad-fiber variant is also sufficient:
for some alpha,beta>0,
sum_{t:q_t>B^(-alpha)} m_t
 <=
B^(-beta+o(1))*M3.
Then
P/M3
 <=
B^(-alpha)+B^(-beta+o(1)).
This is a genuinely weaker moving-base theorem than the Stage35-style requirement that every endpoint fiber be empty.
It also matches the repository same-measure support/moment philosophy: high-survival fibers may be allowed provided their M3 mass is exceptional.
MOVING_FIBER_UNIFORM_EMPTINESS_REQUIRED=false.
MOVING_FIBER_AVERAGE_ENERGY_SUFFICIENT=true.
#### Current blocker
Need a uniform theorem over moving rational t, for example:
- specialization-new point control;
- uniform congruence distribution;
- two-fibration incidence/energy estimate;
- quantitative height distribution across occupied fibers.
This route could implement either R1 or R2, so it remains live.
---
### R4. ABSOLUTE NUMERATOR ROUTE
Goal species:
P(B)=o(B^(1/3)).
If achieved, certified M3(B)>>B^(1/3) immediately gives P/M3->0.
Current global upper available from Stage29:
P(B) <<_epsilon B^(1/2+epsilon).
Thus the remaining absolute exponent gap is roughly 1/6 in power.
Two main implementations have been explored.
#### R4a. Terminal-cover / accumulating-curve geometry
Status: LIVE_PARTIAL.
Let
pi:S->K_c
be the endpoint degree-two cover.
Load-bearing result from ARCHIVE Section37:
for any fixed integral degree-6 curve C subset K_c,
the restricted cover pi^{-1}(C)->C is non-split.
Because the full endpoint surface S has no integral degree-6 curves,
a fixed degree-6 M3 curve contributes at most
O(B^(1/6+o(1)))
endpoint points.
Hence every fixed finite collection of such curves is negligible against M3(B)>>B^(1/3).
This is a genuine numerator exponent improvement on a nontrivial subpopulation.
Unfinished:
- classify all denominator-scale accumulating curve families on K_c;
- count the number of relevant curves/families under physical height;
- control the Zariski-dense residual population.
Important: this is one implementation of R4, not the overall primary route.
#### R4b. Ambient four-variable double-square analysis
Status: BLOCKED_ELEMENTARY; REOPEN_ON_STRONGER_ANALYTIC_INPUT.
Exact assets:
- four-variable E4/H4 model;
- endpoint = simultaneous E4 square and H4 square;
- complete local mixed character sums;
- generic additive Fourier transform O(p^2);
- squarefree incomplete-box bound.
Negative result:
standard double square sieve + elementary completion reaches only about
B^(4/5+epsilon)
in the balanced physical regime.
This is far weaker than both the desired B^(1/3) scale and the already known global P<<B^(1/2+epsilon).
Do not continue elementary completion.
Reopen only for materially stronger input such as q-van der Corput / determinant-method / dimension-reducing structure.
---
### R5. DENOMINATOR-UPGRADE ROUTE
Status: UNTESTED_BROAD / NO_CURRENT_MECHANISM.
Current certified:
M3(B) >> B^(1/3),
P(B) <<_epsilon B^(1/2+epsilon).
Therefore a theorem of the form
M3(B) >> B^(1/2+delta)
for some delta>0
would immediately imply P/M3->0.
ARCHIVE history:
- the B^(1/3) lower came from freeing the hidden two-dimensional parameter space in the generalized Saunderson construction;
- a direct search for a further B^(1/2)-scale family was attempted;
- one promising divisibility ansatz collapsed to a sparse involutive pattern and did not add parameter dimension.
No theorem rules out a stronger denominator construction in principle.
But no currently retained mechanism is close to the required >1/2 exponent.
Keep as UNTESTED, not as a primary route.
---
### R6. ONE-FACTOR / GAUSSIAN COMMON-CORE ROUTE
Status: BLOCKED_AS_PRIMARY; AUXILIARY_ARSENAL.
Exact receiver:
L_- square
is sufficient for endpoint membership and therefore proving its M3-density zero would close P/M3.
Large exact arsenal accumulated:
- canonical terminal factors L_-,L_+;
- primitive norm rectangle N_-,N_+;
- common core h;
- Gaussian reconstruction;
- squarefree kernel K_-;
- root lines modulo h and h^2;
- fixed X,D,N_-,N_+,(h,J) multiplicity B^o(1);
- small-coordinate M3-negligibility.
But repeated attempts returned to:
local/fixed-packet information
  ->
moving exact M3 population.
Ambient h-lattice summation and root-line counting were proved insufficient.
Therefore no further equivalent L_- coordinate or local-symbol refinement gets primary effort.
Reopen only if a genuinely new relative same-M3 counting theorem appears.
---
### R7. CANONICAL IMAGE-SIZE / FIXED-FIBER COMPRESSION
Status: BLOCKED_FOR_EXISTING_COORDINATES.
Existing fixed fibers:
t, X, D, N_-, N_+, (h,J)
have B^o(1) multiplicity.
The full endpoint identities were tested against:
X, D, N_-, N_+, h, K_-, (h,J), N_-/N_+, t.
No exact mechanism was found forcing endpoint values into only B^(1/3-epsilon) coordinate values.
This route is frozen for those coordinates.
Reopen only when a new independent structural theorem creates a genuinely smaller image.
---
### R8. JOINT V4 MODEL
Status: EQUIVALENT_SUPPORT_TO_R1.
Exact cells on selected two-face incidence host:
third NO, space NO   : M2-N2
third NO, space YES  : N2
third YES, space NO  : 3*(M3-P)
third YES, space YES : 3P.
Stage29 already merged this route into the terminal P/M3 kernel because no separate global joint theorem survived.
Keep the V4 model as the exact bookkeeping/correlation language for R1.
It becomes a separate LIVE route only if a genuinely new joint theorem appears that is not merely a reformulation of terminal selector correlation.
---
### R9. TORIC / COMMON-HOST BRANCH-PROFILE COMPARISON
Status: LIVE_SUPPORT_TO_R1.
Stage28 established:
- same two-face toric base;
- same cover degree;
- same branch class -2K_Y;
- same K3 type;
- same physical base height;
- distinct branch support:
  space = 4 rational components,
  third-face = 2 genus-1 components;
- first-order local sieve dimensions equal;
- relative local Euler product has no polynomial or first-order log drift.
Thus any terminal interaction saving must come from finer arithmetic:
- branch position/factorisation;
- rational/adelic lift distribution;
- accumulating curves/multisections;
- exact primitive/canonical selector correlation.
This is currently the most concrete geometric implementation of R1.

The first route to deepen should be R1, because:

4. Stage28 already isolated the correct theorem species: centered same-measure selector correlation;
5. unlike the absolute numerator route, it does not require beating B^(1/3) absolutely;
6. unlike the one-factor route, it keeps the full endpoint condition.
This is a selection by expected information gain, not a theorem that R1 is the unique or ultimately best route.

- existing canonical image-size coordinates without a new theorem.
Before opening a new route, state which top-level closure mechanism R1–R9 it belongs to and what exact missing implication it advances.
Different coordinates alone do not constitute a new route.

## 10. R1 depth test — exact terminal interaction determinant and covariance
### 10.1 Exact 2x2 population table
Work on the exact selected-two-face incidence host counted by
I2 = M2 + 3*M3.
Let F be the indicator that the missing third face is also integral, and S the indicator that the space diagonal is integral.
The exact cell counts are:
F=0,S=0 : M2-N2
F=0,S=1 : N2
F=1,S=0 : 3*(M3-P)
F=1,S=1 : 3*P.
Therefore
Pr(F=1)=3*M3/I2,
Pr(F=0)=M2/I2,
Pr(S=1|F=0)=N2/M2,
Pr(S=1|F=1)=P/M3.
This uses exactly the Stage29 selected-incidence multiplicity. No V4 sheet multiplicity or independence assumption is introduced.
### Theorem 10-A — TerminalInteractionDeterminant
Define
D_terminal(B) := M2(B)*P(B) - M3(B)*N2(B).
Then exactly
P/M3
 = N2/M2
 + D_terminal/(M2*M3).
Since the audited Stage24 theorem gives
N2/M2 -> 0,
we have the exact equivalence
P/M3 -> 0
iff
D_terminal/(M2*M3) -> 0.
Because P/M3 and N2/M2 are nonnegative, it is already sufficient to prove the one-sided estimate
max(D_terminal,0)=o(M2*M3).
A large negative interaction defect cannot obstruct the target density-zero conclusion.
### Theorem 10-B — TerminalCovarianceIdentity
On the same selected-incidence probability measure,
Cov(S,F)
 = E[SF]-E[S]E[F]
 = 3*D_terminal/I2^2.
Moreover
Pr(F=1)*Pr(F=0)
 = 3*M2*M3/I2^2.
Hence exactly
P/M3 - N2/M2
 = Cov(S,F)/[Pr(F=1)*Pr(F=0)].
Therefore
P/M3 -> 0
iff
Cov(S,F)/[Pr(F=1)*Pr(F=0)] -> 0.
Since M3=o(M2), one has Pr(F=0)->1, so equivalently
P/M3 -> 0
iff
Cov(S,F)/Pr(F=1) -> 0.
For a one-sided closure it suffices that
Cov(S,F)_+ = o(Pr(F=1)).
This is the minimal centered same-measure receiver currently known.
### 10.2 Multiplicative terminal interaction receiver
When N2>0 define
T_terminal
 := (P/M3)/(N2/M2)
 = P*M2/(M3*N2).
The audited Stage24 upper gives, for every epsilon>0,
N2/M2 <<_epsilon B^(-1/2+epsilon)*(log B)^(-5).
Consequently any theorem of the form
T_terminal << B^(1/2-delta)
for some fixed delta>0 is sufficient for P/M3->0.
In particular
T_terminal = B^o(1)
would be more than enough.
This target is substantially weaker than proving asymptotic independence T_terminal->1.
### 10.3 Relation to historical interaction work
Stage25 already proved a nontrivial earlier interaction ratio
I_25=(N2/M2)/(N1/M1)
and showed it diverges positively.
Thus one must not assume that adding more face conditions leaves space survival asymptotically unchanged.
The new terminal receiver does not assume such independence. It asks only that the terminal enhancement from exactly-two faces to three faces grow slower than the available B^(1/2) survival decay.
This is why the multiplicative target T_terminal << B^(1/2-delta) is the correct weak receiver rather than T_terminal->1.
### 10.4 Arsenal / StructureRadar depth test
The closest retained theorem species is SR-STR-169: same-measure signed physical-selector correlation.
Historical StructureRadar work reduced its own Stage27 measure to a precise covariance/quadratic-form deficit gate, but did not prove the theorem. It was eventually frozen as WORK_ONLY / external theorem gate to prevent further missing-lemma renaming.
The supporting weapons have the following current role:
- SR-STR-161: quadratic/Jacobi large sieve after exact coefficient separation;
- SR-STR-164: square-lift collision-cover/sieve architecture;
- SR-STR-165: Gaussian quadratic-Hecke large sieve after separation/conductor cleanup;
- SR-STR-166: charged-once eliminant routing;
- SR-STR-173: same-measure support/moment and no-recharge firewall.
None currently supplies Theorem10-B on the selected-two-face M3 population.
Cross-promotion from Stage27's H_phys^MAIN measure is forbidden without a new exact adapter.
Therefore:
### Audit Result 10-C — R1IsGenuineNewTheoremGate
R1 is not already solved by the retained Arsenal.
Its exact missing theorem is now smaller than a full same-M3 equidistribution theorem:
TerminalSelectedIncidenceNormalizedCovarianceDecay:
Cov(S,F)_+ = o(Pr(F=1))
on the exact primitive/canonical selected-two-face incidence measure under R<=B.
Any stronger estimate implying
T_terminal << B^(1/2-delta)
also closes the target.
### 10.5 Strategic consequence
R1 has now passed the depth test in the following sense:
- exact receiver derived: YES;
- exact population/measure adapter: YES;
- known theorem already closes it: NO;
- historical supporting weapons exist: YES;
- new global theorem/adapter still required: YES.
This makes R1 a genuine Class-3-style theorem gate, not another coordinate reformulation.
It still does not prove R1 is ultimately easier than R2/R3/R4a/R5.
PRIMARY_ROUTE remains UNDECIDED until at least one other LIVE route is depth-tested to the same standard.

3. strongest retained theorem;
4. first genuinely missing theorem;
5. quantitative strength required to close P/M3;
6. whether an existing Arsenal weapon reaches that strength.

## 11. Comparable-depth audit of R2 / R3 / R4a / R5
Purpose: compare the remaining LIVE/UNTESTED routes at the same depth standard used for R1.
No route is promoted merely because its notation is cleaner.
### 11.1 R2 — exact M3 growing local-square sieve
#### Exact minimal receiver
For a finite set of good primes S, let A_S(B) be the exact primitive/canonical M3(B) objects whose space quantity is locally a square at every p in S.
Every endpoint lies in A_S(B).
The local Master-Hit theorem already gives nontrivial squareclass contraction at each good prime; in the simplest form the one-prime conditional acceptance is
1/2 + O(p^(-1/2)).
Therefore full fixed-prime equidistribution is stronger than necessary.
A sufficient closure theorem is:
### Receiver R2-MIN — ExactM3GrowingLocalSquareUpperSieve
There exists a family of good-prime sets S(B), with #S(B)->infinity, such that
#A_{S(B)}(B) / M3(B) -> 0,
with exact primitive/canonical/R<=B/one-object-one-count semantics.
Any quantitative upper sieve of the shape
#A_S(B)/M3(B)
 <= exp(-c*#S)+o(1)
for a slowly growing admissible S already suffices.
#### Adapter status
Exact global Master-Hit coverage: proved.
Local conditional character law: proved.
Finite-modulus/CRT parameter-side structure: proved.
Physical one-object coordinates and height: proved later in ARCHIVE.
Exact M3 growing-prime transfer: not proved.
#### First genuinely missing theorem
A physical-weighted congruence/large-sieve theorem after conditioning on the third-face square, uniform enough in gcd/squareclass packets to allow #S(B)->infinity.
#### Closure distance
One major theorem gate.
But that theorem is a true same-M3 transfer theorem and has resisted many coordinate changes.
R2_DEPTH_STATUS=ONE_MAJOR_GLOBAL_TRANSFER_GATE.
---
### 11.2 R3 — moving elliptic-fibration base
For the exact rational fibration coordinate t=a2/a3 define
A_t(B)=# M3 points of height <=B on fiber t,
P_t(B)=# endpoint points among them.
Then
M3(B)=sum_t A_t(B),
P(B)=sum_t P_t(B).
Already proved uniformly:
A_t(B)=B^o(1)
for each fixed occupied t, and hence
#T_M3(B) >= B^(1/3-o(1)),
where T_M3(B) is the occupied M3 base image.
#### Exact sufficient receiver
A particularly clean sufficient theorem is:
### Receiver R3-MIN — EndpointActiveBaseImagePowerSaving
Let
T_P(B)={t : P_t(B)>0}.
Prove for some fixed delta>0
#T_P(B) <= B^(1/3-delta).
Then fixed-fiber B^o(1) gives
P(B) <= B^(1/3-delta+o(1))=o(M3(B)).
This is stronger than logically necessary, but it is a concrete fibration-native theorem that closes the target.
A weaker weighted alternative is any uniform moving-base theorem implying directly
sum_t P_t(B)=o(sum_t A_t(B)).
#### Adapter status
Exact physical t-map: proved.
Elliptic/genus-one fiber model: proved.
Physical height formula: proved.
Fixed-fiber bounded-height count: proved.
Polynomially many occupied base values: proved.
Moving-base endpoint distribution: not proved.
#### First genuinely missing theorem
A uniform theorem controlling specialization-new endpoint points across rational t, or a base-image power saving for endpoint-active fibers.
Historical moving-fiber work reaches the same boundary: individual Chabauty/MW calculations do not satisfy the moving-base quantifier.
Closest retained theorem species: moving compatible-small-point/fiber-product gates such as SR-STR-223, still external.
R3_DEPTH_STATUS=ONE_BROAD_UNIFORM_MOVING_FAMILY_GATE.
---
### 11.3 R4a — terminal cover / accumulating curves
#### Exact minimal architecture
Decompose the exact physical M3 population on K_c into
A(B) = points carried by accumulating/low-degree subvarieties,
G(B) = residual Zariski-dense population.
Then it is enough to prove separately
P_A(B)=o(B^(1/3)),
P_G(B)=o(B^(1/3)).
#### Existing exact progress
For every fixed integral degree-6 curve C subset K_c, the endpoint cover is non-split and its endpoint lift contributes at most
O(B^(1/6+o(1))).
Therefore every fixed finite collection of such curves is already harmless.
#### First genuinely missing theorems
Two distinct obligations remain:
1. an accumulating-subvariety classification/count strong enough to sum over every relevant curve family, not merely a fixed finite collection;
2. a rational-point/counting theorem for the residual endpoint-cover population.
The second obligation may be attacked by determinant/geometric-sieve methods only after the first is controlled.
#### Closure distance
At least two global layers remain.
This route has the best current exponent on a fixed geometric subpopulation, but it is not the shortest whole-population route.
R4A_DEPTH_STATUS=TWO_LAYER_GLOBAL_COUNTING_GATE.
---
### 11.4 R5 — denominator upgrade
Known:
P(B) <<_epsilon B^(1/2+epsilon).
Therefore if one proves for some fixed delta>0
M3(B) >> B^(1/2+delta),
then choosing epsilon<delta gives immediately
P(B)/M3(B)->0.
#### Exact minimal receiver
### Receiver R5-MIN — SuperHalfPowerM3Lower
Construct or count an exact primitive/canonical M3 subpopulation with
#F(B) >> B^(1/2+delta)
for some fixed delta>0.
#### Existing progress
The current B^(1/3) lower arose by exposing the hidden two-dimensional parameter freedom in the generalized Saunderson construction.
ARCHIVE explicitly investigated a possible next dimension gain through a U1|U2 divisibility ansatz. The tested relation produced only sparse hits and an involutive pairing, not a new free parameter family.
No theorem rules out another stronger family.
But no retained construction currently approaches exponent >1/2.
#### First genuinely missing theorem/construction
A new global M3 family or counting mechanism with parameter-dimension / physical-height exponent ratio strictly above 1/2, together with bounded multiplicity and primitive/canonical adapters.
R5_DEPTH_STATUS=NEW_MECHANISM_NOT_YET_IDENTIFIED.

## 12. Comparable-depth conclusion
After bringing R1,R2,R3,R4a,R5 to the same standard:
R1 Terminal population interaction:
- exact minimal receiver: YES;
- population adapter: YES;
- historical theorem progress: GREEN;
- remaining layers: one same-measure covariance theorem.
R2 Same-M3 local-to-global:
- exact minimal receiver: YES;
- local input: strong/proved;
- population adapter: partial but exact geometry available;
- remaining layers: one major growing-prime physical transfer theorem.
R3 Moving fibration:
- exact minimal receiver: YES;
- fixed-fiber theory: strong/proved;
- remaining layers: one broad uniform moving-family theorem.
R4a Terminal cover:
- fixed subpopulation exponent: strong/proved;
- remaining layers: accumulating-family total + residual count, at least two layers.
R5 Denominator upgrade:
- closure implication: trivial/exact;
- current construction approaching required exponent: NO;
- remaining layer: discover a new high-dimensional M3 mechanism first.
### Current route ordering by logical closure distance
This is not a theorem about mathematical difficulty.
It is only a current research-routing statement:
1. R1 — shortest exact implication chain and strongest prior theorem progress;
2. R2 — equally fundamental, but the physical-transfer theorem is broader;
3. R3 — one theorem gate, but moving-family quantifier is broad;
4. R4a — two whole-population layers remain;
5. R5 — potentially decisive but mechanism still unidentified.
R1 should therefore receive the next bounded research unit.
R2 and R3 remain LIVE and must not be erased or called dominated.
R4a remains LIVE_PARTIAL.
R5 remains UNTESTED.
PRIMARY_ROUTE remains formally UNDECIDED until R1 either gains a genuine estimate or hits a frozen theorem gate without a new attack.

If no exact adapter appears, freeze R1 at its precise theorem gate and rotate to R2 rather than reformulating it again.

## 13. R1 preflight result — directional raw-pair host removes the aggregate multiplicity issue
### 13.1 Exact directional host
Stage25's audited raw-pair completion adapter gives, for each unique shared-edge direction j in {a,b,c},
H_j(B) := P_j(B) = M2,j(B) + M3(B).
An exactly-two-face object belongs to exactly one shared-edge chamber j.
An M3 object contributes exactly once to every direction j.
Stage24 gives
M2,j(B) ~ C_j*B*(log B)^5,
C_j>0,
and
N2,j(B)/M2,j(B)
 <<_{epsilon,j}
 B^(-1/2+epsilon)*(log B)^(-5)
 ->0.
The Stage25 lower family also gives directionwise
N2,j(B) >>_j B^(1/4),
so
N2,j/M2,j
 >>_j B^(-3/4)*(log B)^(-5).
### 13.2 Directional 2x2 table
On H_j define:
F=1 iff the missing third face is integral,
S=1 iff the space diagonal is integral.
Then exact cell counts are
F=0,S=0 : M2,j-N2,j
F=0,S=1 : N2,j
F=1,S=0 : M3-P
F=1,S=1 : P.
Thus
Pr_j(F=1)=M3/(M2,j+M3),
Pr_j(F=0)=M2,j/(M2,j+M3),
Pr_j(S=1|F=0)=N2,j/M2,j,
Pr_j(S=1|F=1)=P/M3.
No factor 3 appears.
### Theorem 13-A — DirectionalTerminalCovarianceIdentity
Let
D_j := M2,j*P - M3*N2,j.
Then
P/M3 - N2,j/M2,j
 = D_j/(M2,j*M3).
And on the exact H_j probability measure,
Cov_j(S,F)
 = D_j/(M2,j+M3)^2.
Therefore exactly
P/M3 - N2,j/M2,j
 = Cov_j(S,F)/[Pr_j(F=1)*Pr_j(F=0)].
Since
N2,j/M2,j ->0,
for any one fixed direction j,
P/M3 ->0
iff
Cov_j(S,F)/[Pr_j(F=1)*Pr_j(F=0)] ->0.
Because M3=o(M2,j), Pr_j(F=0)->1, so a one-sided sufficient receiver is
Cov_j(S,F)_+ = o(Pr_j(F=1)).
This is strictly cleaner than the aggregate I2=M2+3M3 formulation because it removes direction-mixture covariance and the Euler multiplicity factor 3.
### 13.3 Directional terminal interaction factor
Define
T_j(B)
 :=
 [P/M3] / [N2,j/M2,j]
 = P*M2,j/(M3*N2,j)
whenever N2,j>0.
Then
P/M3 = T_j * (N2,j/M2,j).
Since the directional space-survival upper is
N2,j/M2,j
 << B^(-1/2+epsilon)*(log B)^(-5),
any bound
T_j << B^(1/2-delta)
for a fixed delta>0 suffices.
In particular T_j=B^o(1) is sufficient.
The current purely unconditional information gives only the crude upper
T_j << B^(3/4+o(1)),
using P/M3<=1 and the directional lower N2,j/M2,j >> B^(-3/4)(log B)^(-5).
Thus the quantitative interaction gap is now explicit:
current generic interaction ceiling exponent <= 3/4;
closure threshold requires exponent < 1/2.
A power improvement of more than 1/4 in the terminal interaction factor would close the M3 density goal.
### 13.4 Relation to the Stage25 causal interaction ladder
Stage25 already studies the previous interaction cross-ratio
I_2
 = (N2/N1)/(M2/M1)
 = (N2/M2)/(N1/M1),
and proves positive divergent enhancement.
The terminal factor is the exact next rung:
I_3,j
 = (P/N2,j)/(M3/M2,j)
 = (P/M3)/(N2,j/M2,j)
 = T_j.
Hence R1 is naturally a terminal condition-interaction problem, not an independence problem.
The earlier result warns that T_j->1 must not be assumed.
The actual M3 target only requires the much weaker growth restriction
T_j << B^(1/2-delta).
### 13.5 Common-host adapter status
The directional H_j host is the same labeled shared-edge two-face geometry used by the Stage18/24 toric model:
Y=Bl_4(P1 x P1),
with exact physical anticanonical height R.
On its dense physical chart the two marginal square predicates are exactly:
third-face square,
space square.
Thus the population/height side of the R1 adapter is already exact at the directional level.
The remaining missing object is analytic, not semantic:
a same-H_j-measure correlation theorem for the two square-lift selectors.
The retained SR-STR-169 family is the closest theorem species, but no source-locked theorem proves the required covariance deficit on this exact H_j measure.
SR-STR-161/165 require an additional low-rank/separated-coefficient representation that is not currently proved for this selector pair.
SR-STR-173 supplies only the same-measure/no-recharge firewall.
### 13.6 R1 anti-loop disposition
R1 has reached a stable exact theorem boundary:
R1_POPULATION_ADAPTER=true
R1_DIRECTIONAL_RAW_PAIR_HOST=true
R1_HEIGHT_ADAPTER=true
R1_MINIMAL_COVARIANCE_RECEIVER=true
R1_REQUIRED_INTERACTION_EXPONENT_LT_ONE_HALF=true
R1_EXISTING_ANALYTIC_THEOREM_MATCH=false
Therefore normal algebraic reformulation of R1 should stop here.
R1_STATUS=LIVE_THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
Reopen R1 only on one of:
- a new same-measure selector-correlation theorem;
- an exact separation adapter making SR-STR-161/165 applicable;
- a direct proof T_j << B^(1/2-delta);
- a new identity that genuinely lowers the required exponent.
Under the Cycle Exploration Safety Protocol, rotate rather than rename the missing lemma.

## 14. R2 re-entry check — later physical divisor/delta work does not discharge the transfer gate
Purpose: test the explicit re-entry condition left by Section13.
The question is not whether Sections32–35 improved the physical model. They did.
The question is whether they supplied a genuinely new theorem that transports the local square law to the exact M3 measure strongly enough for Receiver R2-MIN.
### 14.1 What the later work repaired
ARCHIVE Sections32–35 prove:
1. exact odd-edge divisor coordinates
   A,C | X^2
   with one-object/one-count semantics;
2. exact primitivity:
   gcd(d_X(A),d_X(C))=1;
3. exact Euler third-face condition;
4. exact physical height:
   (A^2+C^2)(X^4+A^2 C^2)
   <= 4 A^2 C^2 B^2;
5. removal of the invalid old raw Master-Hit square-box truncation;
6. an integral physical bidegree-(2,2) model;
7. restricted signed squarefree descent with
   delta_odd | rad(PQ);
8. only (PQ)^o(1) squareclass/allocation slices per physical packet.
Thus the semantic/height/multiplicity side of the old R2 wall is substantially cleaner than in the original Master-Hit formulation.
### 14.2 What was NOT obtained
ARCHIVE Section35 explicitly records that a fixed-delta slice is not automatically toric:
after the individual conic parametrizations are imposed together with the base identities, a genus-1 core remains.
Most importantly, ARCHIVE Section38 states the remaining theorem in essentially the same form as R2-MIN:
> prove fixed-modulus / finite-prime equidistribution, or a strong relative sieve inequality,
> for the exact primitive/canonical physical M3 population after the Euler condition,
> with error summable over the squareclass/gcd slices.
Its recommended next step was still:
- fix q and signed squarefree delta;
- parameterize the conic;
- retain the coupled genus-1 condition and exact height;
- prove a fixed-q counting statement uniform in delta and cross-gcd labels;
- only then sum the (PQ)^o(1) slices.
No such fixed-q uniform physical counting theorem appears in the later retained work.
### Theorem 14-A — R2ReentryNegativeCertificate
The later physical divisor/delta descent changes the coordinates and removes several semantic obstacles, but it does not change the theorem class of the first missing global input.
In particular, none of the retained results proves Receiver R2-MIN:
#A_{S(B)}(B)/M3(B) -> 0
for any growing good-prime set S(B).
Nor do they prove the weaker fixed-q relative-error theorem needed to begin that summation.
Therefore the Cycle Exploration re-entry condition for R2 is NOT met.
R2_STATUS=THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
R2_REENTRY_FROM_DIVISOR_DELTA=false.
### 14.3 Exact frozen gate
The narrowest retained R2 gate is:
R2_FIXED_Q_DELTA_UNIFORM_PHYSICAL_COUNT:
For fixed good modulus q, obtain an exact primitive/canonical/R<=B count on each retained delta/cross-gcd slice with a relative error uniform enough to sum over the (PQ)^o(1) physical slice family.
A theorem of this type would reopen R2.
Another coordinate reformulation, another local character calculation, or another fixed-delta genus computation does not reopen it.
### 14.4 Rotation
Under the anti-loop protocol, rotate rather than rename this gate.
R1 is already LIVE_THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
R2 is now THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
R3 has a materially changed weaker receiver from Section3/11:
sum_t p_t(B)^2/m_t(B)=o(M3(B)),
or an exceptional M3-mass theorem for high endpoint-survival fibers.
This average-energy receiver is strictly weaker than the uniform moving-fiber theorem previously used to judge R3.
Hence a bounded R3 re-entry check is justified.

## 15. R3 re-entry check — average fiber energy is weaker, but no retained theorem currently controls it
Purpose: test whether the weaker Receiver R3-A materially changes the applicability of the existing moving-fiber arsenal.
### 15.1 The weaker receiver is genuine
For the K_c fibration coordinate t used by the M3 anti-concentration theorem, write
m_t(B)=#M3_t(B),
p_t(B)=#P_t(B).
Then
P/M3
 =
sum_t [m_t/M3]*[p_t/m_t].
By Cauchy-Schwarz,
(P/M3)^2
 <=
(1/M3) * sum_t p_t^2/m_t.
Hence
R3_AVERAGE_ENERGY:
sum_t p_t^2/m_t=o(M3)
is sufficient.
Likewise an exceptional-mass theorem for high q_t:=p_t/m_t is sufficient.
This is strictly weaker than uniform emptiness of every endpoint fiber.
### 15.2 Stage35 does not directly supply this theorem
Stage35's selected direct endpoint fibration uses the full endpoint surface and the rational parameter
t35=(e+d)/z.
Every physical endpoint enters that fibration, but a general M3 point has no rational space-diagonal coordinate d.
Therefore t35 is not an exact fibration coordinate on the full M3 denominator population.
The M3 anti-concentration coordinate used in R3 is instead the rational K_c fibration coordinate inherited from the Euler-brick surface.
No retained exact adapter identifies the Stage35 endpoint-only base measure with the M3-weighted base measure
m_t/M3
required by Receiver R3-A.
Thus Stage35's fixed-fiber genus-5 geometry, bad-fiber ledger, elliptic quotients and receiver compression cannot be promoted directly to the R3 average-energy theorem.
### 15.3 Fixed-fiber B^o(1) is insufficient by itself
The proven bound
m_t(B)=B^o(1)
uniformly for fixed occupied t implies many occupied base values.
But since p_t<=m_t,
p_t^2/m_t <= p_t,
so the trivial use of the fixed-fiber bound gives no saving beyond the numerator P itself.
Likewise knowing only that each fiber contains B^o(1) M3 points does not imply that the M3 mass of high-survival fibers is exceptional.
A genuinely moving-base correlation/moment theorem is still needed.
### 15.4 What would reopen R3
The narrowest useful re-entry theorem is not uniform point exclusion.
Either of the following suffices:
R3_ENDPOINT_FIBER_ENERGY:
sum_t p_t^2/m_t=o(M3);
or, for fixed alpha,beta>0,
R3_HIGH_SURVIVAL_EXCEPTIONAL_MASS:
sum_{t:p_t/m_t>B^(-alpha)} m_t
 <=
B^(-beta+o(1))*M3.
A two-fibration incidence/energy estimate implying either statement would also reopen R3.
No retained Stage35 / M3 fibration result currently proves one of these.
### Theorem 15-A — R3ReentryNegativeCertificate
The weaker average-energy receiver materially lowers the required quantifier compared with Stage35 uniform emptiness, but the current retained arsenal still lacks the necessary M3-weighted moving-base moment theorem.
Therefore:
R3_STATUS=THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
R3_REENTRY_FROM_STAGE35=false.
This does NOT say the average theorem is as difficult as the Stage35 uniform theorem.
It only says no existing retained theorem closes it.
### 15.5 Rotation
R1, R2 and R3 have now each reached a precise theorem gate without a current source-locked estimate.
The next materially distinct route is R4a, terminal-cover / accumulating-subvariety geometry.
Unlike R1-R3, R4a seeks an absolute numerator bound and already has a fixed-subpopulation exponent 1/6.
The bounded re-entry test is whether the available K_c / endpoint-surface curve classification is strong enough to control the NUMBER of relevant accumulating curves, rather than only each fixed curve separately.

## 16. R4a re-entry check — fixed-curve saving does not yet sum over the K_c accumulating spectrum
Purpose: test whether the retained Testa-Stoll / endpoint-cover geometry closes the family-count layer left in Section11.
### 16.1 What is actually classified
The imported Testa-Stoll theorem completely classifies integral curves of canonical/projective degree <=6 on the FULL endpoint surface S.
In the positive nondegenerate physical chamber, no endpoint curve family of canonical degree <=6 survives.
This is a theorem on S.
It is not a classification of all degree-6 curves on the Euler K3 denominator K_c.
### 16.2 What Section37 legitimately transfers
For any fixed integral degree-6 curve C subset K_c, if the restricted endpoint double cover split generically, a degree-6 component would appear on S.
Testa-Stoll forbids that.
Hence each fixed degree-6 C has a nonsplit degree-2 endpoint lift of degree 12, and contributes at most
B^(1/6+o(1))
endpoint points.
This fixed-curve theorem is valid and retained.
### 16.3 The unresolved summation layer
To turn the fixed-curve estimate into a whole accumulating contribution, one needs a theorem controlling the collection of relevant C as B grows.
No retained result proves that:
- only finitely many physical degree-6 curves C subset K_c can carry M3 points;
- or only B^o(1) such curves are relevant up to height B;
- or the total endpoint contribution after summing all such curves is o(B^(1/3)).
The Testa-Stoll endpoint-surface classification cannot be reversed to obtain such a K_c curve-family count.
Stage29-14 explicitly keeps the distinction:
low-degree endpoint carriers are classified, but higher-degree carriers, isolated points and unresolved low-genus quotient classes remain outside that closure.
### 16.4 The residual layer also remains
Even if every accumulating curve family were controlled, the residual Zariski-dense endpoint-cover population would still require a separate rational-point theorem.
No current retained theorem gives
P_residual(B)=o(B^(1/3))
under the exact physical height.
### Theorem 16-A — R4aReentryNegativeCertificate
The fixed degree-6 exponent B^(1/6+o(1)) is a genuine strong local geometric saving, but the current arsenal does not supply the two global summation layers required to turn it into a whole-population endpoint bound.
Therefore:
R4A_STATUS=LIVE_PARTIAL_THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
Re-entry requires at least one material new input:
1. a classification/count of denominator-side accumulating curves with a summable family bound; or
2. a theorem directly bounding the union of their endpoint lifts; or
3. a residual rational-point theorem strong enough that the accumulating part can be isolated and summed separately.
Another fixed-curve degree/genus calculation does not reopen R4a.
### 16.5 Route-breadth consequence
R1: exact covariance receiver, theorem gate paused.
R2: exact growing local-sieve receiver, theorem gate paused.
R3: weaker average-energy receiver, theorem gate paused.
R4a: strong fixed-curve saving, two-layer global theorem gate paused.
R5: closure by denominator upgrade remains mechanism-free.
A distinct blind-pass candidate has not yet received comparable-depth treatment:
DIRECT_PHYSICAL_INCIDENCE:
work directly with the occupied physical (X,D) image of M3 and the fourth Pythagorean condition
X^2+D^2=R^2,
seeking an M3-aware incidence/determinant/energy theorem rather than ambient root-line counting.
This is not automatically equivalent to the blocked h-root-line route, because it need not condition on one-factor/common-core data.
It must therefore remain UNTESTED rather than being silently absorbed.

## 17. R10 preflight — direct physical (X,D) incidence is distinct but needs an occupied-image expansion theorem
Purpose: test the blind-pass route that ignores one-factor/common-core coordinates and works directly with the fourth Pythagorean condition.
### 17.1 Exact physical image
For a canonical M3 object let
X = unique odd edge,
D = sqrt(Y^2+Z^2).
Define the occupied physical image
W_B := {(X,D): some E in M3(B) has these coordinates}.
Define its endpoint-active subset
E_B := {(X,D) in W_B : X^2+D^2 is an integer square}.
The endpoint condition is exactly the displayed Pythagorean relation.
### 17.2 Fiber multiplicity
The retained fixed-(X,D) theorem gives uniformly
mu_B(X,D)
 :=
#{E in M3(B): (X(E),D(E))=(X,D)}
 <=
B^o(1).
Therefore
|W_B| <= M3(B) <= B^o(1)|W_B|,
and
P(B) <= B^o(1)|E_B|.
Hence:
### Receiver R10-A — OccupiedPhysicalPairIncidenceDeficit
Any fixed delta>0 such that
|E_B| <= B^(-delta+o(1)) |W_B|
implies
P(B)/M3(B)->0.
This is a direct physical incidence receiver.
### 17.3 Why ambient Pythagorean counting is insufficient
For fixed X, the endpoint relation factors as
(R-D)(R+D)=X^2,
so the number of possible endpoint D values for that fixed X is divisor-many.
But the M3 image itself also has only B^o(1) pairs above each fixed X, by fixed-X anti-concentration.
Thus low degree of the ambient Pythagorean graph does not create a relative saving on W_B.
The same issue occurs after fixing D.
Ambient lattice/root-line counting is also too large compared with the certified M3 denominator and was already audited in the h-root-line route.
### 17.4 Missing genuinely new theorem
To make R10 effective one needs structure of the OCCUPIED M3 pair image, for example:
- a product/expansion theorem forcing many D-values per relevant X-scale;
- an incidence-energy deficit between W_B and the Pythagorean graph;
- a determinant-method theorem retaining the M3 witness equations;
- a two-coordinate equidistribution theorem on W_B.
No retained theorem supplies such an expansion/incidence statement.
The separate fixed-X and fixed-D B^o(1) results do not imply it.
### Theorem 17-A — R10PreflightDisposition
R10 is materially distinct from the one-factor h-root-line route because it uses the full endpoint condition directly and does not charge the common-core restriction.
However its first missing quantitative input is again a new whole-population incidence theorem on the exact M3 image.
Therefore:
R10_STATUS=UNTESTED_THEOREM_GATE.
Do not mark it BLOCKED by R6/R7.
Do not prioritize it over a route with an existing theorem-shaped adapter unless a new occupied-image expansion mechanism is identified.
### 17.5 Breadth-audit checkpoint
The blind-pass route inventory has now been reconciled as follows:
- terminal population interaction -> R1;
- Master-Hit/global torsor/local-global -> R2;
- moving fibration -> R3;
- terminal-cover / low-degree curve spectrum -> R4a;
- absolute ambient double-square -> R4b;
- denominator/height growth -> R5;
- primitive norm/common-core -> R6;
- image-size/fixed-fiber -> R7;
- V4/cohomological cross language -> R8/R1 support;
- common toric host/adelic branch comparison -> R9/R1 support;
- direct physical incidence -> R10.
No blind-pass candidate has been silently discarded.
Automorphism/quotient-only routes are support routes unless an exact population/height adapter appears.
At this checkpoint:
R1_STATUS=LIVE_THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED

# PRE-CLEANUP CURRENT MAIN SNAPSHOT — CROSS-GCD SECTION37 BOUNDARY


# K16-C3-M3-LOCAL-TO-GLOBAL — MAIN
## Current research authority / active route
> このファイルだけ読めば通常の M3 研究を再開できる状態を保つ。
> 旧主ルート・詳細証明・negative result・監査前の MAIN 全文は K16-C3-M3-LOCAL-TO-GLOBAL-ARCHIVE に保存済み。
---
## 0. M3研究の現在のゴール
対象は primitive canonical Euler brick の exact physical population M3(B)。
必須条件:
- primitive
- canonical ordering
- Euclidean height R<=B
- one object = one count
- 3 face diagonals integral
endpoint subset を P(B) とする。
現在の唯一の主ゴール:
P(B)/M3(B) -> 0.
完全直方体の非存在そのものは、現在の M3 研究のゴールに含めない。
既知の certified denominator scale:
M3(B) >> B^(1/3).
したがって absolute endpoint count で閉じるなら
P(B)=o(B^(1/3))
で十分。ただし relative M3 argument なら absolute B^(1/3) barrier を直接越える必要はない。
## 0.5 Research credit / routing firewall
- 研究 credit は厳格に分離する。有限計算 ≠ global theorem、numerical / receiver / theorem / effectivity / endpoint credit は別物。
- population、measure、field、quotient、model、height、multiplicity 等が変わるなら exact adapter 必須。
- 同じ restriction/saving を二重に credit しない。独立性を勝手に仮定しない。
- formal class / orbit / cohomology があるだけでは、必要な geometric object の存在証明にならない。
- blocked route や有限範囲で hit=0 は impossibility ではない。
- perfect-cuboid の existence/nonexistence は explicit audited full-endpoint certificate がない限り絶対に claim しない。
- Stage-local controller はこの firewall を強化してよいが、弱めてはいけない。
- 既存武器を探すときだけ repository-asset-discovery、route を広げる・park・reopen・exhausted 判定をするときだけ cycle-exploration-safety-protocol を開く。
---
## 1. Exact physical endpoint structure
Canonical labeling:
- X = unique odd edge
- Y,Z = even edges
- P^2=X^2+Y^2
- Q^2=X^2+Z^2
- D^2=Y^2+Z^2
- R^2=X^2+Y^2+Z^2
M3 conditionは D integer。
endpoint conditionはさらに R integer。
Exact terminal factorization:
(PQ-YZ)(PQ+YZ)=X^2 R^2.
G=gcd(PQ,YZ),
L_-=(PQ-YZ)/G,
L_+=(PQ+YZ)/G.
L_-,L_+ は positive odd coprime。
Endpoint iff
L_- and L_+ are both integer squares.
one-factor condition L_-=square は endpoint の canonical necessary conditionだが、監査後は primary global route ではない。
---
## 2. Primitive norm architecture — auxiliary load-bearing arsenal
Exact primitive Gaussian/norm decomposition:
T:=R^2=N_- N_+,
h:=gcd(N_-,N_+).
Endpoint では exact に
N_-=h*u^2,
N_+=h*v^2,
gcd(u,v)=1,
したがって
R=h*u*v.
one-factor では N_-/h square のみを要求する。
Current retained physical consequences:
- gcd(h,X)=1
- h^2 | X^2+D^2
- D/X is a Gaussian root of -1 mod h^2
- fixed (X,D) physical multiplicity is B^o(1)
これらは auxiliary structure として保持するが、moving-h root-line counting 単独では relative M3 closure を与えないことは監査済み。
---
## 3. Accepted anti-concentration arsenal
以下は再証明しない。
### 3.1 Fixed t fiber
K_c の rational fibration coordinate t について、各 fixed occupied t-fiber は
N_t(B)<=exp(O(log B/log log B))=B^o(1)
uniformly。
したがって occupied t-values は少なくとも
B^(1/3-o(1))
個必要。
B^(1/3-epsilon) 個以下の t-fibers の集合は M3-negligible。
### 3.2 Other fixed-coordinate fibers
既存 exact results により fixed:
- X
- D
- N_-
- N_+
- (h,J), J=(X^2+D^2)/h^2
はいずれも relevant M3 fiber multiplicity B^o(1)。
small-X, small-D, small-N_-, small-N_+, small-C_- については B^(1/3-epsilon) threshold 以下の領域が M3-negligible になる exact anti-concentration results を保持。
これらは今後 accumulating-curve decomposition の補助武器として使用する。

## 4. Root receiver after route-breadth audit
The route audit is complete enough to separate the GOAL-NORMALIZING RECEIVER from the possible PROOF METHODS.
PRIMARY_METHOD=UNDECIDED.
The current M3 goal remains
P(B)/M3(B) -> 0.
The most useful exact normalization comes from one directional selected-two-face host.
Fix one missing-face direction j.
Let
M2,j = exactly-two-face objects in direction j without the space condition,
N2,j = the corresponding space-diagonal survivors.
On the exact directional host
H_j = M2,j disjoint_union M3,
define Boolean selectors
F=1 iff the missing third face is integral,
S=1 iff the space diagonal is integral.
Then the exact 2x2 cell table is
F=0,S=0 : M2,j-N2,j
F=0,S=1 : N2,j
F=1,S=0 : M3-P
F=1,S=1 : P.
Therefore
Pr_j(S=1|F=0)=N2,j/M2,j,
Pr_j(S=1|F=1)=P/M3.
The audited directional Stage24 bounds give
N2,j/M2,j
 << B^(-1/2+epsilon)(log B)^(-5)
 ->0,
and also a lower corridor of order at least
B^(-3/4)(log B)^(-5).
Define
D_j := M2,j*P - M3*N2,j.
Then exactly
P/M3 - N2,j/M2,j
 = D_j/(M2,j*M3),
and
Cov_j(S,F)
 = D_j/(M2,j+M3)^2.
Hence
### ROOT RECEIVER — TerminalDirectionalCovariance
P(B)/M3(B)->0
iff
Cov_j(S,F) /
[Pr_j(F=1)Pr_j(F=0)]
 ->0
for any one fixed direction j.
Because M3=o(M2,j), a sufficient one-sided form is
Cov_j(S,F)_+ = o(Pr_j(F=1)).
Equivalently define the terminal interaction factor
T_j(B)
 :=
[P/M3]/[N2,j/M2,j]
 =
P*M2,j/(M3*N2,j).
Any estimate
T_j(B) << B^(1/2-delta)
for one fixed delta>0 is sufficient.
Current unconditional information gives only
T_j(B) << B^(3/4+o(1)).
Thus the quantitative interaction gap is explicit:
current generic ceiling exponent <=3/4;
closure threshold requires exponent <1/2.
This receiver is NOT itself a proof method and does NOT assert independence.
ROOT_RECEIVER=TerminalDirectionalCovariance.
TERMINAL_INTERACTION_THRESHOLD_PROVED=false.

## 5. Proof-method ledger
The following are materially distinct methods for attacking the root receiver or the original ratio.
The labels below are current MAIN authority. Historical R1–R10 details are preserved in ARCHIVE.
### METHOD A — common-host selector correlation
Historical aliases:
old R1 + R8 + R9.
Exact host:
the directional selected-two-face physical host is the same labeled toric/del-Pezzo geometry used in Stage18/24,
Y=Bl_4(P1 x P1),
with the audited physical anticanonical height.
Two degree-two square-lift selectors live on this same host:
- missing-third-face completion;
- space-diagonal completion.
Known exact support:
- same base;
- same physical height;
- both degree-two covers;
- both branch class -2K_Y;
- joint V4 cover / cross quotient language;
- branch decompositions differ:
  space = 4 rational components,
  third-face = 2 genus-1 components.
The directional population/height adapter is already exact.
Missing theorem:
a same-H_j-measure correlation estimate giving the root covariance deficit, or directly
T_j << B^(1/2-delta).
Closest retained theorem species:
SR-STR-169 same-measure selector correlation.
Supporting routers:
SR-STR-161 separated quadratic large sieve,
SR-STR-165 Gaussian Hecke large sieve,
SR-STR-166 charged-once eliminant,
SR-STR-173 same-measure support/moment firewall.
No exact separation/transfer currently makes one of these quantitative theorems applicable.
Status:

Reopen only for:

- an exact coefficient-separation adapter;
- a charged-once eliminant with genuinely new determinant;
- a direct T_j exponent <1/2 theorem.
Do not continue by merely renaming the covariance.
### METHOD B — exact M3 local-to-global / Master-Hit
Historical alias:
old R2/R2b.
Known:
- exact global Master-Hit coverage;
- controlled parameter multiplicity;
- strong local conditional squareclass law;
- finite-modulus/CRT parameter-side structure;
- exact odd-edge divisor one-object coordinates;
- exact primitivity;
- exact physical height;
- integral bidegree-(2,2) model;
- signed squarefree delta descent;
- only (PQ)^o(1) squareclass/allocation slices.
Critical correction retained:
the old raw Master-Hit square-box truncation was invalid; only the exact physical coordinates may be used.
Later divisor/delta work did NOT discharge the global theorem gate.
The narrowest frozen gate is:
R2_FIXED_Q_DELTA_UNIFORM_PHYSICAL_COUNT:
for fixed good q, count the exact primitive/canonical/R<=B population on each retained delta/cross-gcd slice with relative error uniform enough to sum the (PQ)^o(1) slice family.
No retained theorem proves that estimate.
METHOD_B_STATUS=THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
Reopen only when an actual fixed-q/delta uniform physical count or equivalent relative large-sieve theorem appears.
### METHOD C — moving-fibration average energy
Historical alias:
old R3.
For the exact K_c rational fibration coordinate t define
m_t(B)=#M3_t(B),
p_t(B)=#P_t(B).
Known uniformly on every fixed occupied fiber:
m_t(B)=B^o(1).
Hence M3 occupies at least B^(1/3-o(1)) base values.
The M3 goal has the exact weighted form
P/M3
 =
sum_t [m_t/M3]*[p_t/m_t].
By Cauchy-Schwarz,
(P/M3)^2
 <=
(1/M3) * sum_t p_t^2/m_t.
Thus the following is sufficient:
### Receiver C1 — MovingFiberEndpointEnergy
sum_t p_t(B)^2/m_t(B)
 =
o(M3(B)).
A good/bad variant is also sufficient:
if for some alpha,beta>0,
sum_{t:p_t/m_t>B^(-alpha)} m_t
 <=
B^(-beta+o(1))*M3,
then P/M3->0.
This is strictly weaker than uniform endpoint emptiness on every fiber.
Stage35 does not directly supply it:
its selected direct endpoint fibration uses an endpoint-only parameter involving the space diagonal, so it is not an exact fibration of the whole M3 denominator population.
Fixed-fiber B^o(1) alone also does not imply the energy bound.
METHOD_C_STATUS=THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
Reopen for:
- an M3-weighted moving-base second-moment theorem;
- an exceptional-base-mass theorem;
- a two-fibration incidence/energy estimate implying one of them.
### METHOD D — terminal cover / accumulating-subvariety geometry
Historical alias:
old R4a.
Let
pi:S->K_c
be the endpoint degree-two cover.
Known strong fixed-subpopulation theorem:
for every fixed integral degree-6 curve C subset K_c, the restricted endpoint cover is non-split.
Its dominating lift has degree 12, so one fixed C contributes at most
B^(1/6+o(1))
endpoint points.
Hence any fixed finite collection is negligible against
M3(B)>>B^(1/3).
But Testa-Stoll classifies low-degree curves on the endpoint surface S, not all denominator-side degree-6 curves on K_c.
Two global layers remain:
1. classify/count the relevant accumulating curve families on K_c strongly enough to sum their endpoint lifts;
2. bound the residual Zariski-dense endpoint population.
METHOD_D_STATUS=LIVE_PARTIAL_THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED.
Reopen for:
- a summable denominator-side accumulating-curve classification/count;
- a direct bound for the union of their endpoint lifts;
- a residual rational-point theorem under the exact physical height.
### METHOD E — denominator upgrade
Historical alias:
old R5.
Known numerator upper:
P(B) <<_epsilon B^(1/2+epsilon).
Therefore any theorem
M3(B) >> B^(1/2+delta)
for one fixed delta>0 would immediately prove the goal.
Current best certified lower is only B^(1/3), arising from a generalized Saunderson-type two-parameter mechanism.
No retained construction approaches an exponent strictly above 1/2.
METHOD_E_STATUS=UNTESTED_MECHANISM_FREE.
Do not spend primary effort here until a genuinely new high-dimensional M3 family/counting mechanism is identified.
### METHOD F — direct occupied physical (X,D) incidence
Historical alias:
old R10.
Define
W_B := {(X,D): some E in M3(B) has these coordinates},
E_B := {(X,D) in W_B: X^2+D^2 is an integer square}.
Fixed-(X,D) multiplicity is B^o(1), hence a fixed-power occupied-image deficit
|E_B| <= B^(-delta+o(1))|W_B|
would imply P/M3->0.
Ambient Pythagorean sparsity alone is insufficient:
for fixed X the endpoint relation has divisor-many D, but the M3 image itself also has only B^o(1) pairs over fixed X.
Thus a new theorem must use the occupied M3 pair image itself:
- incidence-energy deficit;
- determinant method retaining the M3 witness equations;
- expansion/product structure of W_B;
- or a two-coordinate distribution theorem.
METHOD_F_STATUS=UNTESTED_THEOREM_GATE.
This route is distinct from the blocked common-core h-root-line route and must not be silently absorbed into it.

## 10. Cross-route continuation — METHOD B receiver correction and finite-prime terminal criterion
This continuation replays ARCHIVE Sections6–8, 106, 109–113 against the current proof-method ledger.
The purpose is to distinguish what METHOD B actually needs from stronger historical receivers.
### 10.1 Exact local input already available
On the complete good-prime Master/half-angle residue measure conditioned on the Euler/M3 square predicate, Section6 proves for every sufficiently large good odd prime p
Pr(space radicand is a nonzero square mod p | Euler radicand is a nonzero square mod p)
 = 1/2 + O(p^(-1/2)),
with an absolute implied constant.
Section7 then gives exact CRT factorization for every fixed finite set S of good primes in that complete parameter residue measure.
The later four-variable exact physical model writes
E4(a,b,c,f)
 = c^2*f^2*(b^2-a^2)^2
   + a^2*b^2*(f^2-c^2)^2,
H4(a,b,c,f)
 = (a^2*f^2+b^2*c^2)
   *(a^2*c^2+b^2*f^2),
with
M3 iff E4 is an integer square,
endpoint iff E4 and H4 are both integer squares,
and the exact physical cutoff
H4 <= 4*gcd(ab,cf)^2*B^2.
Thus the local square bit to be transferred is already attached to the correct terminal polynomial H4. No moving terminal gcd twist is needed on the product route.
### 10.2 Correction to the historical fixed-prime receiver
ARCHIVE Sections109/110 proposed, for fixed good p, a receiver of the form
sum_{E in M3(B)} chi_p(H4(E)) = o(M3(B)).
This is a sufficient cancellation statement, but it is NOT the correct minimal expected fixed-p theorem.
Reason:
Section6 only proves that the complete local conditional character bias is O(p^(-1/2)).
For fixed p this is a fixed constant depending on p and need not equal zero.
Therefore physical fixed-p transfer should not be required to force the character mean to zero.
It is enough to reproduce, or merely upper-bound by, a local acceptance strictly below 1.
Accordingly:
HISTORICAL_FIXED_PRIME_ZERO_MEAN_RECEIVER=OVERSTRONG_NOT_MINIMAL.
No claim is made here that the zero-mean statement is false for every p; only that it is not implied by the local theorem and is unnecessary for the M3 density goal.
### 10.3 Fixed finite prime sets are already terminally sufficient
For a finite set S of good odd primes define
A_S(B)
 :=
 #{E in M3(B): H4(E) is a square modulo p for every p in S}.
Every perfect endpoint belongs to A_S(B), for every S.
Hence
P(B)/M3(B) <= A_S(B)/M3(B).
The crucial quantifier order is:
1. fix S;
2. let B->infinity;
3. only after taking the B-limit, enlarge S.
Therefore no modulus q(B) growing with B is logically required.
### Theorem 10-A — FixedFinitePrimeContractionIsTerminal
Suppose there exist a constant rho<1 and arbitrarily large finite sets S_k of good primes with |S_k|=k such that
limsup_{B->infinity}
 A_{S_k}(B)/M3(B)
 <= rho^k.
Then
P(B)/M3(B)->0.
Proof:
for every fixed k,
limsup P/M3 <= rho^k.
Let k->infinity after the B-limsup. Since rho^k->0, the limsup is zero. QED.
A more source-matched sufficient statement is:
for every fixed finite S of sufficiently large good primes,
limsup_{B->infinity}
 A_S(B)/M3(B)
 <=
 product_{p in S}
 [1/2 + C*p^(-1/2)],
for one absolute C and after absorbing the O(1/p) zero-residue strata into C*p^(-1/2).
Choosing all p large enough makes every factor <=rho<1, so Theorem10-A applies.
### 10.4 New minimal METHOD B receiver
The correct terminal-strength target is therefore not growing-prime uniformity.
It is:
### Receiver B-MIN — ExactM3FixedFinitePrimeContraction
For arbitrarily large fixed finite sets S of sufficiently large good odd primes, prove on the exact primitive/canonical/one-object M3 population under R<=B that
limsup_{B->infinity}
 # {E in M3(B): local endpoint-square condition holds at every p in S}
 / M3(B)
is bounded by rho^{|S|} for some fixed rho<1.
Full equidistribution to the complete-residue product law is stronger than necessary.
A one-sided contraction bound is enough.
Equivalent useful theorem species:
- fixed-q physical upper sieve for q=product(S);
- fixed-S conditional equidistribution on M3;
- iterative same-M3 conditional contraction at each p in a fixed list;
- fixed-q residue-class count with constants allowed to depend arbitrarily on q, provided the limiting acceptance retains exponential decay in |S|.
Crucially:
GROWING_Q_WITH_B_REQUIRED=false.
FIXED_FINITE_PRIME_PHYSICAL_TRANSFER_TERMINAL_SUFFICIENT=true.
### 10.5 What remains hard after this weakening
The hard step is still genuine.
The exact M3 population is the E4-square locus with:
- primitive masks;
- canonical inequalities;
- gcd-dependent physical height;
- sparse arithmetic support;
- equivalently, the Section35 delta/cross-gcd decomposition with only (PQ)^o(1) slices but a coupled genus-one core.
No retained theorem currently proves even the fixed-q physical contraction after summing those slices.
Thus METHOD B is narrowed but not closed:
complete local residue contraction
 -> fixed finite q
 -> exact physical M3 contraction
remains the missing bridge.
This is weaker than the formerly emphasized growing-prime Selberg/large-sieve theorem and should replace it as the first METHOD B target.
### 10.6 Literature cross-route screen: Jonathan Love 2025 near-match
Jonathan R. Love, Rational configuration problems and a family of curves, Journal of Number Theory 269 (2025), studies the family
y^2
 =
 [a(1-x^2)+2bx]^2
 +
 [c(1-x^2)+2dx]^2
and proves a density-zero theorem for ambient parameter matrices giving locally soluble / rationally soluble curves.
There is an exact formal near-match on one K_c t-fiber.
Fix
t=a2/a3,
a3=1,
a2=t,
a1=(1-u^2)/(2u).
Since on K_c
b1^2=1+t^2,
the space condition c_space^2=a1^2+b1^2 becomes
(2u*c_space)^2
 =
 (1-u^2)^2
 +
 (2*b1*u)^2.
This is Love's displayed family with the special diagonal parameter matrix
diag(1,b1).
However this does NOT transfer Love's density-zero theorem to M3:
- this diagonal subfamily always has the obvious rational point u=0 on the compactified curve;
- hence it lies in a special exceptional subfamily rather than the generic ambient matrix family on which local obstructions create density zero;
- the M3 denominator additionally imposes the independent genus-one third-face condition
  w^2=u^4+(4t^2-2)u^2+1;
- Love's parameter height/measure is not the exact M3 physical R<=B measure.
Therefore:
LOVE_2025_DIRECT_M3_TRANSFER=false.
LOVE_2025_FORMAL_FIBER_MATCH=true.
This is retained as a useful negative adapter certificate, not theorem credit.
### 10.7 Bonolis--Browning remains methodology-only
ARCHIVE Section112 already audited Bonolis--Browning's hyperelliptic-fibration square-sieve method.
For fixed (a,c,f), E4=W^2 is quadratic in x=b^2 and even quartic in b, whereas their published theorem requires odd hyperelliptic degree n>=3.
Therefore the published theorem remains non-applicable off the shelf.
Its square-sieve/q-van-der-Corput architecture is relevant only if the needed even-quartic incomplete sums are reproved.
No new credit is assigned here.
### 10.8 Updated METHOD B status
METHOD_B_STATUS=THEOREM_GATE_PAUSED_NARROWED.
METHOD_B_GROWING_MODULUS_REQUIRED=false.
METHOD_B_FIXED_FINITE_PRIME_CONTRACTION_TERMINAL_SUFFICIENT=true.
METHOD_B_HISTORICAL_ZERO_MEAN_RECEIVER_MINIMAL=false.
METHOD_B_PHYSICAL_FIXED_Q_TRANSFER_PROVED=false.
This makes METHOD B logically closer to closure than the previous wording suggested, but no existing theorem yet crosses its exact physical-measure gate.
NEXT_ITEM=FixedQPhysicalM3ContractionMechanismSearch
The next bounded unit should search for an exact fixed-q argument in the Section32/110 physical coordinates before returning to broad moving-modulus technology.
Potential mechanisms to test, in order:
1. finite automorphism/involution pairing that preserves physical height and mixes local endpoint states;
2. fixed-q lattice equidistribution after the Section35 delta peel, with q fixed before B->infinity;
3. third-Pythagorean-triple torsor coordinates plus fixed-q geometry-of-numbers;
4. only if these fail, external same-K3 adelic equidistribution theorems.
METHOD_B_RECEIVER_CORRECTED=true
LOVE_2025_NEARMATCH_AUDITED=true
P_OVER_M3_LIMIT_PROVED=false

## 11. METHOD C continuation — M3-defined same-X double-divisibility receiver
This section returns to the denominator-defined K_c fibration and imports only later Stage35/36 weapon species that match it exactly.
No endpoint-only base is used.
No generic-rank or fixed-fiber theorem is re-credited when it was already proved in Stage14/ARCHIVE.
### 11.1 Exact M3-compatible genus-5 fiber
Use the Euler-K3 coordinates
b1^2=a2^2+a3^2,
b2^2=a1^2+a3^2,
b3^2=a1^2+a2^2.
Fix the rational fibration coordinate
t=a2/a3.
On the affine chart a3=1 write
a2=t,
a1=(1-u^2)/(2u),
b2=(1+u^2)/(2u).
A genuine physical base value is not an arbitrary rational t:
r^2:=1+t^2=b1^2
must already be a rational square.
This is the previously audited spurious-fiber firewall.
The third-face / M3 condition is
w_-^2
 = u^4+(4t^2-2)u^2+1,
where w_-=2u*b3.
The endpoint adds
w_+^2
 = u^4+(4t^2+2)u^2+1,
where w_+=2u*R.
Thus
w_+^2-w_-^2=4u^2.
Now put
xi=u-u^(-1),
eta=u+u^(-1).
Then exactly
eta^2-xi^2=4,
(w_-/u)^2=xi^2+4t^2,
(w_+/u)^2=xi^2+4(1+t^2).
Define
R1=eta=2b2,
R2=w_-/u=2b3,
R3=w_+/u=2R.
The endpoint fiber over a genuine t is therefore the diagonal elementary-2 cover
R1^2=xi^2+4,
R2^2=xi^2+4t^2,
R3^2=xi^2+4r^2,
with r^2=1+t^2.
For generic nondegenerate t the six branch values
xi=+-2i,
xi=+-2it,
xi=+-2ir
are distinct. The degree-8 cover of P1_xi has six order-two branch values, so Riemann-Hurwitz gives genus 5.
This recovers the expected Testa-Stoll genus-5 fiber type, now on the M3-defined base.
### 11.2 Two elliptic quotient curves with the same X
Set
X=xi^2.
From the first two square equations define
Y_t=xi*R1*R2.
Then
Y_t^2=X(X+4)(X+4t^2).
Define the split-full-2 elliptic curve
E_t:
Y^2=X(X+4)(X+4t^2).
Similarly the endpoint third square gives
Y_r=xi*R1*R3
and therefore
E_r:
Y^2=X(X+4)(X+4r^2),
where r^2=1+t^2.
Thus an endpoint point produces points on E_t and E_r with the SAME rational X-coordinate.
After scaling X=4x, Y=8y, E_t becomes exactly Jonathan Love's family
y^2=x(x+1)(x+t^2).
This is an exact curve-family collision with Love 2024, not merely an analogous elliptic family.
Love's root-number/rank-density applications are nevertheless not imported as an M3 counting theorem: they order ambient t-values and use additional conjectural rank inputs, whereas the present population is restricted to genuine Pythagorean t and exact physical M3 height/multiplicity.
### 11.3 Kummer 2-divisibility interpretation
On a full rational 2-torsion curve
E_t:Y^2=X(X+4)(X+4t^2),
the standard Kummer map records the squareclasses
[X], [X+4], [X+4t^2],
with kernel 2E_t(Q) away from the usual torsion/boundary charts.
For the physical M3 point constructed above,
X=xi^2,
X+4=R1^2,
X+4t^2=R2^2.
Therefore its nondegenerate elliptic image satisfies
P_t in 2E_t(Q).
At an endpoint one also has
X+4r^2=R3^2,
so the same X defines
P_r in 2E_r(Q).
Hence:
### Theorem 11-A — M3SameXDoubleDivisibilityForwardMap
Every nondegenerate endpoint on the genuine M3 base t produces a common rational X such that
P_t=(X,Y_t) in 2E_t(Q),
P_r=(X,Y_r) in 2E_r(Q),
r^2=1+t^2.
Every nondegenerate M3 point already produces the first condition P_t in 2E_t(Q).
This is a forward exact adapter.
A full converse including positivity, primitive reconstruction and boundary charts is NOT credited here until separately audited.
### 11.4 Halving the M3 point gives an explicit four-linear endpoint receiver
Let
Q=(x,y) in E_t(Q)
be any rational half of the M3 elliptic point P_t, so
2Q=P_t.
For
E_t:y^2=x(x+4)(x+4t^2),
the duplication formula is
x(2Q)
 =
 (x^2-16t^2)^2
 /
 [4x(x+4)(x+4t^2)].
Since the denominator is (2y)^2, the additional endpoint factor satisfies the exact identity
x(2Q)+4(1+t^2)
 =
 G_t(x)/(2y)^2,
where
G_t(x)
 =
 [x^2+8(t^2-t+1)x+16t^2]
 [x^2+8(t^2+t+1)x+16t^2].
Therefore the endpoint condition is equivalent, on the nondegenerate halving chart, to
G_t(x) being a rational square.
Now parameterize the genuine M3 base by
t=2s/(1-s^2),
r=(1+s^2)/(1-s^2).
Then the two quadratic factors split over Q(s):
x^2+8(t^2-t+1)x+16t^2
 =
 [(x(s-1)^2+8s^2)(x(s+1)^2+8)]/(1-s^2)^2,
x^2+8(t^2+t+1)x+16t^2
 =
 [(x(s-1)^2+8)(x(s+1)^2+8s^2)]/(1-s^2)^2.
The denominator of their product is a square. Thus:
### Theorem 11-B — M3HalvingFourLinearEndpointReceiver
On the nondegenerate genuine-base halving chart, the endpoint condition is equivalent to
G_s(x)
 :=
 [x(s-1)^2+8s^2]
 [x(s+1)^2+8]
 [x(s-1)^2+8]
 [x(s+1)^2+8s^2]
being a rational square.
This is an exact moving four-linear squareclass receiver on the half-point Q.
The factorization and duplication identity were independently symbolically rechecked before entry into MAIN.
### 11.5 What is old and what is new
Already proved before this section and NOT re-credited:
- fixed-fiber genus-one/elliptic structure;
- actual Pythagorean-base restriction;
- generic-rank-zero phenomena for the earlier Stage14 elliptic fibration;
- torsion/boundary analysis on that earlier fibration;
- every fixed M3 t-fiber has B^o(1) physical multiplicity;
- fixed-base terminal genus-5 finiteness;
- global M3 mass requires B^(1/3-o(1)) moving t-values.
New content here:
- the endpoint fiber on the denominator-defined M3 base is written as three simultaneous diagonal quadratic squares;
- the M3 square system gives a split-full-2 elliptic curve E_t with P_t in 2E_t(Q);
- endpoint gives SAME-X 2-divisibility simultaneously on E_t and E_r;
- after halving, the remaining endpoint condition becomes the explicit four-linear square product G_s(x).
No new global density theorem follows yet.
### 11.6 Stage36 weapon rematch
The later provisional Stage36 Arsenal contains close structural weapons:
S36-PW02:
V4 curve/Jacobian quotient decomposition.
Useful for certifying quotient-factor structure; gives no rational-point converse or density theorem.
S36-PW05:
split-full-2 order-4 half and 2-isogeny normalization for
y^2=x(x+a^2)(x+b^2).
The present E_t matches its object species exactly with
a=2,
b=2t.
S36-PW06:
relative 2-isogeny/Kummer specialization baseline via injective specialization and fixed-fiber descent.
Potentially useful after an exact family adapter, but it does not give uniform rank control or receiver closure.
S36-PW03/PW04:
quadratic-extension anti-invariant growth and pointwise elementary-2 lift classes.
These are supporting descent architectures only.
Therefore the later Arsenal materially narrows METHOD C implementation work, but does not close the moving-family count.
### 11.7 New METHOD C minimal receiver
The old wording
moving-fiber theorem
was too broad.
The exact new target is:
### Receiver C-MIN — M3SameXDoubleDivisibilityCorrelation
On the genuine Pythagorean base
t=2s/(1-s^2),
r=(1+s^2)/(1-s^2),
control the exact physical M3-weighted frequency with which an M3 point
P_t in 2E_t(Q)
has the same X-coordinate compatible with
P_r in 2E_r(Q).
Equivalent halving form:
control the exact physical moving-family frequency with which
Q=(x,y) in E_t(Q),
2Q=P_t,
satisfies
G_s(x) in Q^{*2}.
A theorem proving that this compatible subset has o(M3(B)) total weight closes P/M3->0.
This is more specific than generic rank-jump frequency and preserves the denominator-defined base.
