## 11D. Trivial-split cyclic promotion and fixed-fiber H collision
A stronger exact endpoint organization is available in the dedicated route
K16-C3-M3-ROUTE-TRIVIAL-SPLIT-CYCLE
Doc ID: 1_O0DDPJxK1DT6wV2cXm3UqaMcZc7YLFZgKpfQ7dXlaE.
It has now been checked against the current t28/t30/cross-gcd conventions and is promoted as the primary endpoint structure.
Let a primitive endpoint have physical edges e1,e2,e3 and integral space diagonal R.
Define
h_i=gcd(R,e_i).
Then h1,h2,h3 are pairwise coprime.
For the raw edge sharing e_i, let c_i be the Stage14/Master raw-edge cross-gcd.
The two cross-gcd factors are exactly the contents of the other two physical space Pythagorean triangles, hence
boxed:
c1=h2*h3,
c2=h1*h3,
c3=h1*h2.
Therefore
c1*c2*c3=(h1*h2*h3)^2,
and
gcd(c1,c2)=h3,
gcd(c1,c3)=h2,
gcd(c2,c3)=h1.
Put
tau=R/(h1*h2*h3).
For the three trivial-split reduced directions,
D_i=R/h_i,
one has exactly
boxed:
D1=c1*tau,
D2=c2*tau,
D3=c3*tau,
and
boxed:
tau=gcd(D1,D2,D3).
Thus the three one-edge cross-gcd labels are one cyclic endpoint object, not three independent savings.
The t30 cover coordinate also becomes cyclic on an actual endpoint.
For raw direction i, the primitive Euclid pair (p,q) of the physical Kummer ratio is, up to the finite parity/orientation convention, exactly the Euclid pair of another trivial-split direction j.
Equivalently,
(p^2+q^2)/kappa(p,q)=D_j,
where
kappa(p,q)=gcd(p^2-q^2,2pq,p^2+q^2) in {1,2}.
Hence the historical direction -> existential cover witness architecture becomes, on the endpoint subset, a three-direction cycle.
This exact cyclic reuse is stronger than the one-edge cross-gcd tail and stronger than treating the t28 second square as an unrelated extra sieve event.
### M3 exponent split
The generic endpoint ceiling applied at height B^(2/3-eta) gives
P(R<=B^(2/3-eta))
<<
B^(1/3-eta/2+o(1)),
so this small-R endpoint subfamily is already negligible against M3(B)>>B^(1/3).
On the complementary range
R>B^(2/3-eta),
the factorization
R=h1*h2*h3*tau
forces at least one of
h1,h2,h3,tau
to exceed
H=B^(1/6-eta/4).
This gives two exact large-factor branches:
1. large-content:
   some h_i>H, and the same divisor h_i occurs simultaneously in two edge labels c_j,c_k;
2. large-residual:
   tau>H, and the same divisor tau occurs simultaneously in all three reduced direction hypotenuses D1,D2,D3.
Any saving must be proved on this same cyclic endpoint measure.
Do not multiply the two appearances of h_i as independent probabilities.
### Fixed-direction second-square collision theorem
The second-square energy route remains useful inside the cyclic architecture.
For a fixed direction (a,b), put
M0=a^4+6a^2b^2+b^4,
C0=(a^2-b^2)^2,
so
H_{a,b}(T)=M0*(T^4+1)-2*C0*T^2.
Fix a physical first-cover reference state T0 and let
d=H_{a,b}(T0).
Equality of H-squareclasses with the reference is the genus-one curve
Y^2=d*H_{a,b}(T).
A standard binary-quartic Jacobian model is
y^2=x^3-27*I*x-27*J,
with
I=d^2*(12*M0^2+4*C0^2),
J=d^3*(-144*M0^2*C0+16*C0^3).
Its cubic factors exactly as
boxed:
(x-12*d*C0)
(x+6*d*C0-18*d*M0)
(x+6*d*C0+18*d*M0).
Hence the Jacobian has full rational 2-torsion.
The collision curve already has the rational reference point T=T0, so it is an elliptic curve over Q.
All coefficients and all physical point heights are polynomial in the physical B-height.
Therefore the same uniform bounded-height elliptic theorem with rational prime-order torsion used in Stage14-t22/t36 gives
boxed:
fixed direction + fixed H-squareclass
has at most B^o(1) physical first-cover states.
Viewing H with fixed cover slope p/q and moving direction slope a/b gives another even reciprocal quartic, so the reverse fixed-cover collision has the same B^o(1) multiplicity.
Thus row and column H-squareclass collision fibers are polynomially closed.
The unresolved H-energy is genuinely off-direction and off-cover.
This does not by itself prove the global energy bound
Q_H=o(B^(2/3)).
### Cyclic t30/t31/t37 saturation adapter
The cyclic endpoint structure collapses the remaining historical norm-skeleton slack.
For cyclic direction i -> j, with k the remaining index,
D_i=h_j*h_k*tau,
D_j=h_i*h_k*tau,
and the t30 cover Euclid pair satisfies
p_i^2+q_i^2=kappa_i*D_j,
kappa_i in {1,2}.
Every D_i is an odd primitive Pythagorean hypotenuse. Pairwise coprimality of h_1,h_2,h_3 then forces R and all h_i to be odd.
The exact t30 denominator is
delta_i
=
(p_i^2+q_i^2)/gcd(p_i^2+q_i^2,2D_i).
Since gcd(D_i,D_j)=h_k*tau, the parity factor kappa_i cancels exactly and
boxed:
delta_i=h_i.
Since h_i is also the raw-direction physical scale g_i,
boxed:
delta_i=g_i=h_i,
delta_i*D_i=R.
Thus actual endpoints saturate the historical t30/t31 scale divisibility; there is no leftover scale g_i/delta_i.
For an odd canonical direction prime ell_i|D_i,
boxed:
ell_i visible
iff ell_i|D_j
iff ell_i|h_k*tau.
If ell_i is invisible, then its support lies in the nonshared neighboring-content factor:
ell_i|h_j,
ell_i not|h_k*tau.
On a visible t31 branch, the t37 second divisor parameter mathfrak_h_i and common core mathfrak_t_i collapse to
boxed:
mathfrak_h_i=epsilon_j*h_j,
boxed:
mathfrak_t_i
=
[2*g_ij/(epsilon_i*epsilon_j)]
*
[h_k*tau/ell_i],
where g_ij=gcd(epsilon_i,epsilon_j*h_j) in {1,2}.
Hence one large physical content h_j occurs simultaneously as the t31 denominator on edge j -> k and as the t37 reverse parameter on edge i -> j.
This makes the current large-content theorem target precise: the two one-edge square-root-type savings are on the same endpoint cycle and cannot be multiplied without a same-measure two-edge correlation theorem. At H=B^(1/6-o(1)), one such saving alone is insufficient for o(B^(1/3)).
Likewise tau is shared through all cyclic common cores; its three appearances are not independent local savings.
